## Verification Method

- Parsed 811 internal JavaScript, JSX, TypeScript, and repository-tooling files.
- Resolved relative imports, re-exports, literal dynamic imports, the `@/` alias, and the `@proofarena` alias.
- Recorded 3145 direct internal dependency edges.
- Package imports are excluded because this map covers repository-owned architecture.
- A direct import proves file-level dependency. It does not prove that every imported symbol executes at runtime.
